# Ladder climbers — census (LADDER_PARITY_BUILD_NO_KEYS_REQUIRED, owner Q1 2026-10-23)

Owner, verbatim: *"Needs a per-creature evaluation, unfortunately. Many mechs, droids, humans, animals should be able to climb, jump up alone, or slither along a ladder as long as they fit in a 1x1 cell area. Bigger things no, wheeled things no, and many creatures without arms no."*

Rule (the game's own: `RM_LadderParity.TraitsOf` + `RM_LadderParityMath.ClimbEligible`): a per-def `RM_LadderClimber` extension wins; else body size <= 1.2 (setting `ladderMaxClimberBodySize`, PROVISIONAL), no part named wheel/tread/track, and a way up: arms (a `ManipulationLimbCore` part, or humanlike), a jump (`RM_PitTravelExtension.jumps`), or a legless moving body (slithers). In game the size is the pawn's own (life stage), so a calf may climb where its parent cannot.

INSTRUMENT LIMIT: the dump cuts body-part trees past a fixed depth (`<maxdepth:List`1>`, 38148 cut points here), so a part deeper than that (a hand under arm under shoulder) is invisible to this table; arms are read at the shoulder (ManipulationLimbCore), which sits above the cut on every vanilla body checked, but a modded body that puts it deeper reads as armless here and NOT in game (the game reads the whole tree). Treat a 'no arms' row on a humanoid-looking body as a judge case.

Source: `defs.sqlite`, generated by `src/RimMandrake/FlowWorks/Tools/ladder_climber_census.py`. Provenance: captured 2026-10-21T21:46:23Z, 325 mods, load-order fingerprint 4eadba30e450c50e, 58920 defs (refresh.py 2026-10-23: defs.sqlite *current*). Races our own mods added after that capture are not in it.

**2309 race defs; 849 climb, 1460 do not; 435 marked for a judge** (spawn none; a judge agent rules each and the ruling becomes an `RM_LadderClimber` extension).
